## 14. Mentions

A **mention provider** lets people point at things the system knows with `@` in the chat (a file, a database row, a
note). Declare it with `mention({ title, description, target, of? })`: `target` is the gin type of the key that gets
stored with the message (keep it small and stable: an id, a path), and `of` names the type of the things it points at,
so chips and cards use that type's display and cards offer its methods.

_From `scripts/docs/example/shared/index.ts`:_

```ts
mentions: {
  // `@` in the chat finds notes; chips and cards show them as Note (its label, icon, color and its pin method)
  notes: mention({ title: 'Notes', description: 'Any note, by its title.', target: g.obj({ id: g.text() }), of: types.Note }),
},
```

The server implements three functions, each getting the person who is asking (`ctx.user`, `ctx.session`), so results
are limited to what they may see:

- `search(query, ctx)`: options for what was typed after `@`, each `{ label, hint?, icon?, target }`. The chip shows
  `label`; `target` must fit the declared type.
- `hover(target, ctx)`: the hover card, asked for when hovered so it is current (`{ title, subtitle?, fields?, body?,
  open?, value? }`), or `undefined` when the thing no longer exists. Give `value` as the `of` type to offer its methods.
- `llm(target, ctx)`: the text the LLM gets for the mention: what the thing is, where it lives, and how to reach it
  with the system's fns.

_From `scripts/docs/example/server/impl.ts`:_

```ts
mentions: {
  notes: {
    async search(query, ctx) {
      return (await deps.open(ctx.dataDir).all())
        .filter((note) => matches(note, query, undefined))
        .slice(0, 20)
        .map((note) => ({ label: note.title, hint: note.tags.join(', '), icon: 'note', target: { id: note.id } }));
    },
    async hover(target, ctx) {
      const note = await deps.open(ctx.dataDir).get(target.id);
      if (note === undefined) { return undefined; }
      // returning the value as a Note lets the card offer Note's `pin` method
      return { title: note.title, subtitle: note.tags.join(', '), body: note.text.slice(0, 280), open: { resource: uriOf(note.id) }, value: note };
    },
    async llm(target, ctx) {
      const note = await deps.open(ctx.dataDir).get(target.id);
      if (note === undefined) { return `Note ${target.id} no longer exists.`; }
      return `Note "${note.title}" (id ${note.id}) in the notes system:\n\n${markdownOf(note)}`;
    },
  },
},
```

A custom hover card is a renderer under `mentions` in **The client**; without one the hub draws the `hover` answer.

### Reference: mentions

#### `mention`

function · `import { mention } from '@harness/sdk'`


Refers to: [`QualifiedName`](#core-qualifiedname).

## 15. Actions

An **action** is a typed message the system's renderers send it ("pin", "approve", "load more"). Declare it with
`action({ description, input, returns?, state?, expires? })`: without `returns` it returns nothing, `state` declares
session state fields it reads and writes (like a fn's), and `expires` drops it if it can't be delivered within that
many ms (default: never).

_From `scripts/docs/example/shared/index.ts`:_

```ts
actions: {
  pin: action({ description: 'Pin or unpin a note from its card.', input: g.obj({ id: g.text(), pinned: g.bool() }), returns: g.bool() }),
},
```

The hub **stores every action before delivering it**, so nothing is lost: if the system is offline it waits and is
delivered, in order, when the system connects. A renderer sends one with `view.system.send.pin({ id, pinned })` and gets a
`Delivery` whose `status` is live and whose `result` resolves with the typed result. The server implements each as
`async (input, ctx) => result`; `ctx` has the asker (`ctx.user`, `ctx.session`), the run whose renderer sent it
(`ctx.run`, when there is one), the delivery id, session state, and the run services.

_From `scripts/docs/example/server/impl.ts`:_

```ts
actions: {
  pin: async (input, ctx) => {
    const store = deps.open(ctx.dataDir);
    const note = await store.get(input.id);
    if (note === undefined) { return false; }
    await store.put({ ...note, pinned: input.pinned, updatedAt: deps.now() });
    return true;
  },
},
```

To start a run from a renderer instead (a "run again" button), use `view.system.run.<fn>(args)`: the new run shows in
the chat like any other, with its own approval, status, cost and undo.

### Reference: actions

#### `action`

function · `import { action } from '@harness/sdk'`

Declare an action. Without `returns` it returns nothing.


Refers to: [`Duration`](#core-duration).

#### `ActionDef`

## 16. Model providers

A system can bring **model providers**: models whose calls run in the system (a GPU box, a private API, a gateway the
hub can't reach). Declare each in the contract with `provider({ description, models })`: every model's id, abilities
(aeye capability names such as `chat`, `tools`, `vision`, `embedding`), context window, prices in USD per million
tokens, and whether it reads a prompt cache. The implementation gives a function that makes the aeye `Provider` for the
current config (made again when the config changes).

```ts
// shared/
.with({ providers: { local: provider({ description: 'Models on our GPU box.', models: [
  { id: 'small', capabilities: ['chat', 'tools', 'streaming'], contextWindow: 32_768, pricing: { input: 0, output: 0 }, caching: false },
] }) } })
// server/
serve(contract, { providers: { local: (ctx) => new OpenAIProvider({ apiKey: ctx.config.key, baseURL: ctx.config.url }) } }, options);
```

While the system is connected the hub registers each provider as `<system>/<provider>` (here `gpu/local`), so its models
can be picked like any other; chat calls stream over the link and embeddings are answered by the provider's `embed`. A
call for a model the contract doesn't declare is refused. The hub prices and records every call from the declared
prices, as it does for its own providers.

### Reference: model providers

#### `provider`

function · `import { provider } from '@harness/sdk'`

Declare a provider: `provider({ description: 'Models on our GPU box', models: [{ id: 'small', capabilities: ['chat'],
contextWindow: 8192, pricing: { input: 0, output: 0 }, caching: false }] })`. Each model is checked now, so a wrong
one fails as the contract is built.

```ts
function provider(def: ProviderInput): ProviderDef;
```

#### `ProviderInput`

interface · `import { ProviderInput } from '@harness/sdk'`

What `provider()` takes.

```ts
interface ProviderInput {
  description: string;
  /** Every model it serves. A call for any other model is refused. */
  models: ProviderModelDecl[];
}
```

Refers to: [`ProviderModelDecl`](#core-providermodeldecl).

#### `ProviderDef`

interface · `import { ProviderDef } from '@harness/sdk'`

A provider as the contract declares it.

```ts
interface ProviderDef {
  readonly description: string;
  readonly models: readonly ProviderModelDecl[];
}
```

Refers to: [`ProviderModelDecl`](#core-providermodeldecl).

#### `ProviderImpl`

type · `import { ProviderImpl } from '@harness/sdk'`

The implementation of a declared provider: make the aeye `Provider` for the current config. It is made again when
the config changes, and kept otherwise.

```ts
type ProviderImpl<Cfg = unknown> = (ctx: SystemCtx<Cfg>) => Provider;
```

#### `ProviderRef`

interface · `import { ProviderRef } from '@harness/sdk'`

A handle to a model provider a system brings; the hub registers it under this name.

```ts
interface ProviderRef<N extends QualifiedName> {
  readonly ref: 'provider';
  readonly name: N;
}
```

Refers to: [`QualifiedName`](#core-qualifiedname).

## 17. Resources, embedders and sectioners

### Resources

A system can **serve resources**: things with a URI that the hub lists, indexes, searches, shows and hands to the LLM
(files, pages, records). The implementation's optional `resources` is a `ResourceProvider`:

- `roots()`: the roots it serves (`{ uri, name, description, index: 'all' | 'on-read' | 'none', ignore, watch }`).
- `list(uri, cursor?)`: a page of stats under a URI (`{ items, cursor? }`; `cursor` absent on the last page).
- `stat(uri)`: what is known without reading (`ResourceStat`: name, type, mime, `format` for picking a sectioner
  and a viewer, size, modified time, and a `version` that changes whenever the content does), or `undefined`.
- `read(uri, want)`: `want` lists `text`, `links` and `bytes`; small text goes inline (`{ inline }`), large text and
  bytes by `ContentRef`.
- `watch(uris, onChange)`, optional: report changes until the returned function is called.

URIs are absolute (`notes://all/n1`, `file:///…`) and belong to the system whose root contains them. Every reader
goes through the hub (`ctx.resources.text(uri)` from any system), which routes each URI to the system that serves it.
Build readers on `@aeye/resources` where they fit; the fs system serves folders this way.

The notes system serves each note as markdown under `notes://all/`, so the hub indexes and searches them:

_From `scripts/docs/example/server/impl.ts`:_

```ts
const resources: ResourceProvider = {
  roots: () => Promise.resolve([{ uri: ROOT, name: 'Notes', description: 'Every note, as markdown.', index: 'all', ignore: [], watch: false }]),
  async list(uri) {
    if (uri !== ROOT || current === undefined) { return { items: [] }; }
    return { items: (await current.all()).map(statOf) };
  },
  async stat(uri) {
    const id = idOf(uri);
    const note = id === undefined ? undefined : await current?.get(id);
    return note === undefined ? undefined : statOf(note);
  },
  async read(uri) {
    const id = idOf(uri);
    const note = id === undefined ? undefined : await current?.get(id);
    if (note === undefined) { throw new Error(`There is no note at ${uri}.`); }
    return { stat: statOf(note), text: { inline: markdownOf(note) } };
  },
};
```

### Embedders

An **embedder** is an embed function the hub can index with. Declare it with `embedder({ dimensions, maxInput,
pricePerMillionTokens })` and implement it as `async (texts) => vectors`, one vector of `dimensions` numbers per text,
in order. The embedder system (`systems/embedder`) is the reference.

### Sectioners

A **sectioner** is a way of cutting resources into sections for search and context, offered to people as a choice for
matching resources. Declare it with `sectioner({ description, match })`, where `match` picks resources by `format`,
`mime`, a `glob` over the URI, or a `form` (`table` for any spreadsheet's tables). Implement it as
`async (text, stat) => sections`. Section keys must come from structure (a heading path, a symbol name), never from
position, so they survive edits: make them with `sectionKeys(paths)`, which joins each outline path with ` > ` and
numbers repeats (` ~2`) the same way the hub's own sectioners do.

### Reference: resources, embedders and sectioners

#### `ResourceProvider`

interface · `import { ResourceProvider } from '@harness/sdk'`

A system's resource service: its roots, and listing, stat, reading and watching under them.

```ts
interface ResourceProvider {
  roots(): Promise<ResourceRoot[]>;
  list(uri: ResourceUri, cursor?: string): Promise<Page<ResourceStat>>;
  stat(uri: ResourceUri): Promise<ResourceStat | undefined>;
  /** A read. It may include more than was asked for if it had it anyway. */
  read(uri: ResourceUri, want: ReadWant[]): Promise<ResourceRead>;
  /** Report changes until the returned function is called. */
  watch?(uris: ResourceUri[], onChange: (changes: ResourceChange[]) => void): () => void;
}
```

Refers to: [`Page`](#core-page), [`ReadWant`](#core-readwant), [`ResourceChange`](#core-resourcechange), [`ResourceRead`](#core-resourceread), [`ResourceRoot`](#core-resourceroot), [`ResourceStat`](#core-resourcestat), [`ResourceUri`](#core-resourceuri).

#### `embedder`

function · `import { embedder } from '@harness/sdk'`

Declare an embedder.

```ts
function embedder(def: EmbedderDef): EmbedderDef;
```

#### `EmbedderDef`

interface · `import { EmbedderDef } from '@harness/sdk'`

An embed function the hub can index with.

```ts
interface EmbedderDef {
  readonly dimensions: number;
  /** Most characters per text. */
  readonly maxInput: number;
  readonly pricePerMillionTokens: Usd;
}
```

Refers to: [`Usd`](#core-usd).

#### `EmbedderImpl`

type · `import { EmbedderImpl } from '@harness/sdk'`

An embedder's implementation: one vector per text, in order.

```ts
type EmbedderImpl = (texts: string[]) => Promise<number[][]>;
```

#### `EmbedderRef`

interface · `import { EmbedderRef } from '@harness/sdk'`

A handle to an embedder.

```ts
interface EmbedderRef<N extends QualifiedName> {
  readonly ref: 'embedder';
  readonly name: N;
  readonly dimensions: number;
}
```

Refers to: [`QualifiedName`](#core-qualifiedname).

#### `sectioner`

function · `import { sectioner } from '@harness/sdk'`

Declare a sectioner.

```ts
function sectioner(def: { description: string; match: SectionMatch; }): SectionerDef;
```

Refers to: [`SectionMatch`](#core-sectionmatch).

#### `SectionerDef`

interface · `import { SectionerDef } from '@harness/sdk'`

A way of cutting resources into sections, offered to users as a choice for matching resources.

```ts
interface SectionerDef {
  readonly description: string;
  readonly match: SectionMatch;
}
```

Refers to: [`SectionMatch`](#core-sectionmatch).

#### `SectionerImpl`

type · `import { SectionerImpl } from '@harness/sdk'`

A sectioner's implementation. Keys must come from structure (`sectionKey(path)`), never from position.

```ts
type SectionerImpl = (text: string, stat: ResourceStat) => Promise<Section[]>;
```

Refers to: [`ResourceStat`](#core-resourcestat), [`Section`](#core-section).

#### `SectionerRef`

interface · `import { SectionerRef } from '@harness/sdk'`

A handle to a sectioner.

```ts
interface SectionerRef<N extends QualifiedName> {
  readonly ref: 'sectioner';
  readonly name: N;
}
```

Refers to: [`QualifiedName`](#core-qualifiedname).

#### `sectionKeys`

function · `import { sectionKeys } from '@harness/sdk'`

Make section keys from outline paths, in document order, by `@aeye/resources`' rules (the same keys its markdown
and code slicers give): each key is the path joined with ` > `, `(root)` for an empty path, and a path seen before
gets ` ~2`, ` ~3` so keys stay unique while still coming from structure.

```ts
function sectionKeys(paths: readonly (readonly string[])[]): SectionKey[];
```

Refers to: [`SectionKey`](#core-sectionkey).

## 18. The hub contract

The hub is a system too, named `hub`, declared with the same SDK as the contract `hub` (`import { hub } from
'@harness/sdk'`). Systems use it like any other contract: depend on its fns (`deps: { ask: hub.fns.ask_user }`), give
them to agents (`tools: [hub.fns.search_resources]`), use its types as fields (`hub.types.ContentRef`,
`hub.types.LongText`), declare agents with its `llm` kind (`agent()` does), and refer to its sectioners. The hub's
fns cover searching and loading fns and context, following runs, asking people, resources, context, memory, metrics
and configuration (admin fns are tagged `admin` and run only for admins).

This is the hub contract as the SDK declares it now:

#### Fns of `hub`

- `hub/search_fns`: Find fns by what they do. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    query: text,
    limit?: num{min=1, max=50, whole=true}
  }) => list<obj{fn: text, description: text, available: bool}>
  ```
- `hub/load_fns`: Add fns to your toolset for this session. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{fns: list<text>{minLength=1}}) => obj{
    loaded: list<text>,
    refused: list<obj{fn: text, reason: text}>
  }
  ```
- `hub/drop_fns`: Remove fns from your toolset. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{fns: list<text>{minLength=1}}) => obj{dropped: list<text>}
  ```
- `hub/fns_search`: Search fns and context in the hub by words, meaning or both, best first or by name, filtered by kind, system, instance, who shares them, the types they read or write and their mode, a page at a time. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{ text?, match?, items?, kinds?, systems?, instance?, visibility?, types?, modes?, names?, available?, order?, offset?, limit? … }) => obj{
    items: list<obj{
      item: enum<text>{fn, context},
      name: text,
      system: text,
      description: text,
      kind?: text,
      mode?: enum<text>{read, write, delete},
      signature?: text,
      instance?: text,
      score?: num,
      available: bool,
      unavailable?: text
    }>,
    next?: num{whole=true, min=0}
  }
  ```
- `hub/fn_examples`: A page of a fn's examples (args, the result when kept, and what each shows), the closest to a query first. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    fn: text,
    query?: text,
    offset?: num{whole=true, min=0},
    limit?: num{whole=true, min=1, max=50}
  }) => obj{
    fn: text,
    items: list<obj{args: any, result?: any, note?: text, /* fnExample id */ id: text, score?: num}>,
    total: num{whole=true, min=0},
    next?: num{whole=true, min=0}
  }
  ```
- `hub/fn_get`: One fn as the hub keeps it: its kind, description, instructions, payload (for a fn of a kind), version and where it runs. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    /* The qualified fn name. */ fn: text{minLength=1}
  }) => obj{
    fn: text,
    kind: text,
    description: text,
    instructions?: text,
    payload?: any,
    version: num{min=1, whole=true},
    owner?: text,
    routing?: or<obj{
      to: literal<text>{value="instance"},
      instance: text
    }, obj{to: literal<text>{value="any"}}>,
    available: bool
  }
  ```
- `hub/save_fn`: Keep a fn of one of the calling system's kinds in the hub (a saved form, a saved query), owned by the run's person and shared as asked. Saving a name again keeps the old version in its history. Tags: `own`.

  ```text
  (obj{ kind, name, description, instructions?, payload, examples?, tags?, share?, routing?, replace? … }) => obj{fn: text, version: num{min=1, whole=true}, created: bool}
  ```
- `hub/search_context`: Find context (guides, skills, references) by what it covers. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    query: text,
    limit?: num{min=1, max=50, whole=true}
  }) => list<obj{context: text, description: text}>
  ```
- `hub/load_context`: Load context into this session. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{context: list<text>{minLength=1}}) => obj{loaded: list<text>}
  ```
- `hub/search_resources`: Search files and other resources by meaning and words. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    query: text,
    roots?: list<text>,
    limit?: num{min=1, max=50, whole=true}
  }) => list<obj{uri: text, section?: text, title: text, preview: text, score: num}>
  ```
- `hub/run_status`: The status of a run, with its progress or result. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{run: text}) => obj{
    status: enum<text>{
      "awaiting-approval",
      queued,
      running,
      waiting,
      paused,
      done,
      "invalid-input",
      failed,
      cancelled,
      undone
    },
    progress?: any,
    result?: any,
    error?: obj{
      class: enum<text>{retry, caller, fatal},
      message: text,
      code?: text,
      data?: any
    }
  }
  ```
- `hub/run_wait`: Wait for a run (for example one moved to the background) to finish. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    run: text,
    /* How long to wait, in milliseconds (30000 is 30 seconds). Leave empty to wait as long as this call may run. */ timeout?: duration
  }) => obj{
    status: enum<text>{
      "awaiting-approval",
      queued,
      running,
      waiting,
      paused,
      done,
      "invalid-input",
      failed,
      cancelled,
      undone
    },
    result?: any,
    error?: obj{
      class: enum<text>{retry, caller, fatal},
      message: text,
      code?: text,
      data?: any
    }
  }
  ```
- `hub/ask_user`: Ask a person a question and wait for the answer. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{question: text, user?: text}) => obj{answer: text}
  ```
- `hub/find`: Find fns, context, resources and types by what they are about. Already loaded items are marked. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    query: text{minLength=1},
    kinds?: list<enum<text>{fn, context, resource, type}>,
    limit?: num{min=1, max=50, whole=true}
  }) => list<obj{
    name: text,
    kind: enum<text>{fn, context, resource, type},
    description: text,
    loaded: bool
  }>
  ```
- `hub/inspect`: Look at one item at a detail: a fn's card, a context item's text, a type's definition, or a resource's outline. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    name: text{minLength=1},
    detail?: enum<text>{listed, signature, full}
  }) => obj{
    name: text,
    kind: enum<text>{fn, context, resource, type},
    detail: enum<text>{listed, signature, full},
    text: text
  }
  ```
- `hub/load`: Keep fns (as cards) and context (as text) in front of you from the next step on. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    names: list<text>{minLength=1},
    detail?: enum<text>{listed, signature, full}
  }) => obj{
    loaded: list<text>,
    refused: list<obj{name: text, reason: text}>
  }
  ```
- `hub/unload`: Take fns and context out of what you keep in front of you. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{names: list<text>{minLength=1}}) => obj{
    unloaded: list<text>,
    refused: list<obj{name: text, reason: text}>
  }
  ```
- `hub/resource_outline`: A resource's sections (headings, symbols, pages or tables), each with its key and place in the outline. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{uri: text}) => list<obj{
    key: text{minLength=1},
    path: list<text>,
    title: text,
    start: num{whole=true, min=0},
    end: num{whole=true, min=0},
    embedText?: text,
    form?: enum<text>{markdown, text, code, pages, table, description, ocr, transcript, thumbnail}
  }>
  ```
- `hub/resource_send`: Show a resource (or one section) to yourself at the next step as the model can take it: text, an image, audio or a file. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{uri: text, section?: text{minLength=1}}) => obj{
    parts: list<obj{
      type: enum<text>{text, image, file, audio, video},
      name?: text,
      chars: num{whole=true, min=0}
    }>
  }
  ```
- `hub/mint_mcp_token`: Mint a short-lived token for a hub MCP, for a session this system starts (a Claude session, for one). Only a grantee system's runs may call it. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    /* mcp id */ mcp: text,
    label: text{minLength=1},
    ttl?: num{whole=true, min=1000}
  }) => obj{token: text, expiresAt: timestamp}
  ```
- `hub/resource_list`: List resources (files, pages, uploads) from every system and the hub, filtered, grouped and paged. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{ cursor?, limit?, search?, system?, family?, source?, under?, form?, missing?, group?, in?, query? … }) => obj{ items, cursor?, facets … }
  ```
- `hub/resource_read`: Read a resource as text: its primary form (markdown for pages, tables for spreadsheets) or a chosen form, whole or by sections. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    uri: text,
    form?: or<enum<text>{markdown, text, code, pages, table, description, ocr, transcript, thumbnail}, literal<text>{value="raw"}>,
    section?: text{minLength=1},
    offset?: num{whole=true, min=0},
    maxChars?: num{whole=true, min=1, max=200000},
    rows?: obj{table: text, from: num{whole=true, min=0}, to: num{whole=true, min=0}}
  }) => obj{ uri, form, text, offset, total, truncated, forms, offers … }
  ```
- `hub/context_get`: One context item (a guide, skill, memory or piece of a resource) with its text and its links both ways. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{name: text}) => obj{ record, links, backlinks, text? … }
  ```
- `hub/types_get`: The definitions of named types and of every type they refer to, dependencies first. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{names: list<text>}) => obj{ types, missing … }
  ```
- `hub/runs_list`: Runs, newest first, filtered by fn, status, session or system. A member sees their own runs. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{ cursor?, limit?, query? … }) => obj{ items, cursor? … }
  ```
- `hub/run_get`: One run: its fn, args, status, result or error, cost and timings. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{/* run id */ run: text}) => obj{ id, fn, fnVersion, kind, system, instance?, parent?, root, turn, session?, key, actor, caller, connection?, llmCall?, toolCallId?, args, status, text?, outcome?, progress?, allowed, cost, totalCost, priority, background, attempt, errors, undo?, resume?, stateChanges, tokens?, createdAt, approvedAt?, startedAt?, endedAt? … }
  ```
- `hub/run_tree`: The tree of runs a turn made: each run with the runs it called. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{/* turn id */ turn: text}) => any
  ```
- `hub/llm_call`: One LLM call: its whole request and response, tokens, cost and timings. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{/* llmInteraction id */ id: text}) => obj{ id, turn, run, session?, system?, iteration, provider, model, request, response?, usage?, cost, status, error?, priority, selection?, modelCall?, queuedAt, startedAt?, firstTokenAt?, endedAt? … }
  ```
- `hub/model_calls`: Model calls of every kind (chat, embeddings, images, transcription, speech), newest first, with cost and timings. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{ cursor?, limit?, run?, query? … }) => obj{ items, cursor? … }
  ```
- `hub/fn_metrics`: How fns have been doing: runs, outcomes, retries, durations (p50, p95), queue waits, cost and tokens. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    fn?: text,
    system?: text,
    days?: num{whole=true, min=1, max=365}
  }) => list<obj{ fn, system, from, to, runs, outcomes, p50?, p95?, averageQueued?, averageCost, cost, tokens, retries, lastRunAt? … }>
  ```
- `hub/sessions_list`: Chat sessions, most recent first. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{ cursor?, limit?, query? … }) => obj{
    items: list<obj{
      /* session id */ id: text,
      title: text,
      /* actor id */ owner: text,
      agent: text,
      status: enum<text>{idle, running, paused},
      createdAt: num{whole=true},
      updatedAt: num{whole=true},
      archived: bool
    }>,
    cursor?: text
  }
  ```
- `hub/session_messages`: A session's messages, newest first, paged backwards. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    cursor?: text,
    limit?: num{whole=true, min=1, max=200},
    /* session id */ session: text
  }) => obj{ items, cursor? … }
  ```
- `hub/systems_list`: Every system: whether it is connected, its version, instances and what it offers. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{ query? … }) => list<obj{ name, description, status, enabled, version, manifestHash, defaultInstance?, actor, keyFingerprint, config?, installed?, pairedAt?, pairedBy?, lastSeenAt?, address?, fns, needsConfig, configType?, configSecrets?, instances … }>
  ```
- `hub/system_get`: One system: its status, version, instances, config state and what it offers. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{system: text}) => obj{ name, description, status, enabled, version, manifestHash, defaultInstance?, actor, keyFingerprint, config?, installed?, pairedAt?, pairedBy?, lastSeenAt?, address?, fns, needsConfig, configType?, configSecrets?, instances … }
  ```
- `hub/system_fns`: A system's fns, with whether each can be used now and its running numbers. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{system: text}) => list<obj{ name, system, decl, version, origin, addedBy?, available, paused, disabled, stats, updatedAt … }>
  ```
- `hub/system_logs`: The end of a system's output, oldest first. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    system: text,
    limit?: num{whole=true, min=1, max=2000}
  }) => list<obj{
    at: num{whole=true},
    stream: enum<text>{stdout, stderr, hub},
    text: text
  }>
  ```
- `hub/model_roles`: Which model (and provider) the hub uses for each job: chat, transcription, dictation, speech, describing images, reading text in them. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj) => list<obj{ role, choice?, from, resolved? … }>
  ```
- `hub/model_limits`: Each model's context window, output limit, reasoning reserve and characters per token, with where each came from. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{model?: text}) => list<obj{ model, limits, sources, ratio … }>
  ```
- `hub/query`: Any list (resources, fns, runs, modelCalls, llmCalls, sessions, contexts, notifications, systems, types) by the query standard: free text, filters on the list's fields (is, in, >, <, between, empty, contains, ...), a sort and paging. Tags: `read`.

  ```text
  (obj{ list, query? … }) => obj{
    items: list<any>,
    cursor?: text,
    sort: list<obj{field: text{minLength=1}, dir: enum<text>{asc, desc}}>,
    total?: num{whole=true, min=0},
    /* More rows than the hub reads at once: more may match; narrow the filters for a full answer. */ partial?: literal<bool>{value=true}
  }
  ```
- `hub/resource_convert`: Make a form of a resource now (convert it, describe an image, read the text in it, transcribe audio); it may cost a model call.

  ```text
  (obj{
    uri: text,
    kind: enum<text>{markdown, text, code, pages, table, description, ocr, transcript, thumbnail}
  }) => obj{
    hash: text,
    kind: enum<text>{markdown, text, code, pages, table, description, ocr, transcript, thumbnail},
    converter: text{minLength=1},
    content: obj{hash: text, size: num{whole=true, min=0}, mime: text},
    chars: num{whole=true, min=0},
    tokens: num{whole=true, min=0},
    call?: text,
    createdAt: num{whole=true}
  }
  ```
- `hub/resource_import`: Import a URL or uploaded content as a hub resource, kept and converted by the hub. Tags: `own`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    input: or<obj{
      url: text,
      render?: enum<text>{auto, always, never},
      name?: text,
      folder?: text{minLength=1}
    }, obj{
      content: obj{hash: text, size: num{whole=true, min=0}, mime: text},
      name: text{minLength=1},
      path?: text
    }>
  }) => obj{ uri, system, stat, links, indexedVersion?, sectioner?, source?, raw?, forms?, primary?, seenAt … }
  ```
- `hub/resource_rename`: Rename a hub resource.

  ```text
  (obj{uri: text, name: text{minLength=1}}) => obj{ uri, system, stat, links, indexedVersion?, sectioner?, source?, raw?, forms?, primary?, seenAt … }
  ```
- `hub/resource_delete`: Delete a hub resource; says how many messages, context items and runs still point at it.

  ```text
  (obj{uri: text}) => obj{
    deleted: bool,
    referencedBy: num{whole=true, min=0}
  }
  ```
- `hub/context_from_resource`: Make context items from a resource (one per section, under an outline parent), or update the ones made before.

  ```text
  (obj{
    uri: text,
    sections?: list<text{minLength=1}>,
    copy?: enum<text>{live, snapshot},
    weight?: num{min=0, max=2},
    tags?: list<text{minLength=1}>,
    fns?: list<text>,
    suggestFns?: list<text>,
    alwaysFor?: list<text>,
    command?: text,
    visible?: enum<text>{user, llm, both, none},
    suggest?: enum<text>{user, llm, both, none}
  }) => obj{created: list<text>, updated: list<text>, links: num{whole=true, min=0}}
  ```
- `hub/remember`: Remember something for later as a memory: for this session, the person, or everyone.

  ```text
  (obj{ text, title?, scope?, about?, weight?, summary?, tags?, fns?, suggestFns?, alwaysFor?, command?, visible?, suggest?, parent? … }) => obj{name: text}
  ```
- `hub/forget`: Forget a memory.

  ```text
  (obj{name: text}) => obj{ok: bool}
  ```
- `hub/chat`: The chat agent: answers people in a session with every tool the session allows. A fn of the `hub/llm` kind.

  ```text
  (obj) => text
  ```
- `hub/chat_small`: The chat agent for small local models: the static toolset and the graph contextor, so each prompt fits a small window. A fn of the `hub/llm` kind.

  ```text
  (obj) => text
  ```
- `hub/configure_provider`: Add a model provider (OpenAI, OpenRouter, Ollama) with its API key, or replace its key. The key is stored encrypted. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{ input … }) => obj{models: num{whole=true}}
  ```
- `hub/set_model_category`: Add a model category (a named, ordered list of models), or change the one with this id. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{id?: text, name: text, description: text, models: list<text>}) => obj{/* modelCategory id */ id: text}
  ```
- `hub/set_budget`: Add a spending budget for the hub, a group, a user, a session, a system, a fn or a model, or change the one with this id. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{ id?, target, period, maxCost, maxTokens?, maxCalls?, maxTime?, share?, onLimit … }) => obj{/* budget id */ id: text}
  ```
- `hub/set_priority`: Add a priority rule (the default and highest priority of turns at a level), or change the one with this id. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{ id?, target, default?, max? … }) => obj{/* priorityRule id */ id: text}
  ```
- `hub/set_policy`: Add a policy saying whether actors may run fns (allow, deny, or ask an approver), or change the one with this id. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{ id?, description, subject, target, decision, priority, expiresAt? … }) => obj{/* policy id */ id: text}
  ```
- `hub/set_actor_setting`: Set a setting a system declares for a user, group or agent. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{setting: text, /* actor id */ actor: text, value: any}) => obj{ok: bool}
  ```
- `hub/add_user`: Add a person who can sign in, with their role and groups. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{email: text, name: text, role: enum<text>{admin, member}, groups?: list<text>}) => obj{/* actor id */ id: text}
  ```
- `hub/add_group`: Add a group of people. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{name: text, about: text, members?: list<text>}) => obj{/* actor id */ id: text}
  ```
- `hub/remove_rule`: Remove a budget, priority rule, policy, model category or provider. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{ input … }) => obj{ok: bool}
  ```
- `hub/approve_pairing`: Approve or refuse a system that asked to pair with the hub, optionally naming its instance and setting its config. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    /* pairing id */ pairing: text,
    approve: bool,
    instance?: text,
    config?: any,
    autoPair?: obj{
      expiresIn?: nullable<num{whole=true, min=0}>
    }
  }) => obj{system?: text}
  ```
- `hub/create_pairing_token`: Make a one-time pairing token a new system can pair with, optionally for one system name. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    name?: text,
    expiresIn: num{whole=true, min=0},
    autoPair?: obj{
      expiresIn?: nullable<num{whole=true, min=0}>
    }
  }) => obj{token: text, expiresAt: num{whole=true}}
  ```
- `hub/configure_system`: Set a system's config; refused unless it fits the system's config type. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{system: text, config: any, instance?: text}) => or<obj{version: num{whole=true}}, obj{problems: list<text>}>
  ```
- `hub/enable_system`: Turn a system's functions and its renderers on or off. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{system: text, functions?: bool, renderers?: bool}) => obj{
    enabled: obj{functions: bool, renderers: bool}
  }
  ```
- `hub/remove_system`: Remove a system; its fns become unavailable until it pairs again. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{system: text}) => obj{fnsUnavailable: num{whole=true}}
  ```
- `hub/install_system`: Install a system from a folder, a package or a git repository, and start it. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    source: or<obj{path: text}, obj{package: text, version: text}, obj{git: text, ref: text}, obj{zip: text, file: text}>,
    config?: any,
    autoStart?: bool
  }) => obj{name: text}
  ```
- `hub/start_system`: Start an installed system. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{system: text, instance?: text}) => obj{ok: bool}
  ```
- `hub/stop_system`: Stop an installed system. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{system: text, instance?: text}) => obj{ok: bool}
  ```
- `hub/upgrade_system`: Upgrade an installed system to a version (the latest when none is given). Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{system: text, version?: text}) => obj{from: text, to: text}
  ```
- `hub/set_model_role`: Choose the model (and provider) for a job: chat, transcription, dictation (or the browser's own), speech, describing images, reading text in them. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{ input … }) => obj{ role, choice?, from, resolved? … }
  ```
- `hub/clear_model_role`: Clear a job's model choice, so it falls back to the hub's default (or is off). Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    role: enum<text>{chat, transcribe, dictate, speak, describe, ocr}
  }) => obj{ role, choice?, from, resolved? … }
  ```
- `hub/set_model_limits`: Set a model's context window, output limit, reasoning reserve or characters per token; with every field absent, clear them. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    model: text{minLength=1},
    limits: obj{
      contextWindow?: num{whole=true, min=256},
      maxOutputTokens?: num{whole=true, min=16},
      reasoningTokens?: num{whole=true, min=0},
      charsPerToken?: num{min=1, max=8}
    }
  }) => obj{ model, limits, sources, ratio … }
  ```
- `hub/create_mcp`: Make a hub MCP: an MCP endpoint with its own route and secret, exposing the systems, fns, types, context and resources its rules include. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{ slug, name, instructions, rules, toolMode, user, grantees, origins, enabled … }) => obj{ mcp, secret … }
  ```
- `hub/update_mcp`: Change a hub MCP's name, rules, tool mode or person, or turn it on or off. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{ slug?, name?, instructions?, rules?, toolMode?, user?, grantees?, origins?, enabled?, mcp … }) => obj{ id, slug, name, instructions, rules, toolMode, user, actor, grantees, origins, enabled, createdBy, createdAt, updatedAt, url, sessions, callsToday … }
  ```
- `hub/rotate_mcp_secret`: Give a hub MCP a new secret; every open session of it ends. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{/* mcp id */ mcp: text}) => obj{secret: text}
  ```
- `hub/delete_mcp`: Delete a hub MCP. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{/* mcp id */ mcp: text}) => obj{ok: bool}
  ```
- `hub/preview_mcp`: What draft hub MCP rules would expose now, with warnings, before they are saved. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{ rules … }) => obj{
    fns: list<text>,
    types: list<text>,
    closure: list<text>,
    context: list<text>,
    resources: list<obj{uri: text, system?: text, instance?: text}>,
    reach: list<text>,
    warnings: list<text>
  }
  ```
- `hub/install_package`: Install a system package zip (made by `harness system pack`), pair it, configure it and start it. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{
    zip: obj{hash: text, size: num{whole=true, min=0}, mime: text},
    instance?: text,
    config?: any,
    data?: obj{hash: text, size: num{whole=true, min=0}, mime: text},
    autoStart?: bool
  }) => obj{system: text, instance: text}
  ```
- `hub/remove_instance`: Remove one instance of a system. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{system: text, instance: text}) => obj{ok: bool}
  ```
- `hub/export_system_data`: Zip an instance's data folder with its config (secret fields only when asked) and return the content. Tags: `admin`.

  ```text
  (obj{system: text, instance: text, withSecrets?: bool}) => obj{hash: text, size: num{whole=true, min=0}, mime: text, url: text}
  ```

#### Kinds of `hub`

- `hub/llm`: An agent: an LLM with instructions, tools and context, callable like any fn. Use for work that needs judgement, reading or writing in natural language, or several tool calls.

  ```text
  obj{
    instructions: text,
    model?: or<obj{model: text{minLength=1}}, obj{category: text{minLength=1}}, obj{
      auto: obj{cost?: num{min=0}, speed?: num{min=0}, accuracy?: num{min=0}}
    }>,
    tools: list<text>,
    canLoad: or<literal<text>{value="none"}, obj{fns?: list<text>, systems?: list<text>, tags?: list<text>}>,
    maxTools: num{min=1, whole=true},
    context: list<text>,
    hooks: list<text>,
    toolExecution: enum<text>{parallel, sequential},
    maxIterations: num{min=1, whole=true},
    mode?: or<obj{
      type: literal<text>{value="tools"}
    }, obj{
      type: literal<text>{value="static"},
      protocol?: enum<text>{auto, native, text},
      placement?: enum<text>{"system-end", tail},
      loadDetail?: enum<text>{listed, signature, full}
    }>,
    contextor?: or<obj{
      type: literal<text>{value="plain"}
    }, obj{
      type: literal<text>{value="suggest"},
      topN?: num{whole=true, min=0},
      autoInject?: obj{tokens: num{whole=true, min=0}, items: num{whole=true, min=0}}
    }, obj{
      type: literal<text>{value="graph"},
      metrics?: obj{
        embedding?: obj{weight: num{min=0}, target?: num, k?: num{min=0}},
        words?: obj{weight: num{min=0}, target?: num, k?: num{min=0}},
        turnsAgo?: obj{weight: num{min=0}, target?: num, k?: num{min=0}},
        age?: obj{weight: num{min=0}, target?: num, k?: num{min=0}},
        selectedBefore?: obj{weight: num{min=0}, target?: num, k?: num{min=0}},
        used?: obj{weight: num{min=0}, target?: num, k?: num{min=0}}
      },
      spread?: obj{
        damping?: num{min=0, max=0.95},
        hops?: num{whole=true, min=0, max=4}
      },
      floorExchanges?: num{whole=true, min=0},
      shares?: obj{history: num{min=0}, context: num{min=0}, fns: num{min=0}},
      threshold?: num{min=0},
      evaluate?: enum<text>{perMessage, perIteration}
    }>,
    maxOutput?: num{whole=true, min=16}
  }
  ```

#### Types of `hub`

- `hub/ContentRef`: Stored content by reference: an upload, or an earlier version of a file. Shape: `obj{
  hash: text{pattern="^[0-9a-f]{64}$"},
  size: num{min=0, whole=true},
  mime: text
}`.
- `hub/LongText`: Text that runs over many lines, such as a file body or a plan. Its input is a growing text area. Shape: `text`.

#### Mentions of `hub`

- `hub/fns` (Functions): Any fn the session can use. Target: `obj{fn: text}`.
- `hub/context` (Context): Guides, skills and references. Target: `obj{context: text}`.
- `hub/resources` (Resources): Files and other resources from every system. Target: `obj{uri: text, section?: text}`.

#### Sectioners of `hub`

- `hub/whole`: The whole resource as one section. Matches: `{}`.
- `hub/headings`: One section per heading. Matches: `{"format":"markdown"}`.
- `hub/symbols`: One section per top-level symbol (function, class, type). Matches: `{"glob":"**/*.{ts,tsx,js,mjs,cjs,vue,py,go,rs,java,cs}"}`.
- `hub/pages`: One section per page. Matches: `{"format":"pdf"}`.
- `hub/paragraphs`: One section per paragraph. Matches: `{"format":"text"}`.
- `hub/tables`: One section per table (large tables in row groups, the header repeated). Matches: `{"form":"table"}`.


#### `ADMIN_ENDPOINTS`

const · `import { ADMIN_ENDPOINTS } from '@harness/sdk'`

The web admin endpoints the hub also offers as fns, so whatever an admin configures can be done by asking in chat:
fn name → endpoint. The endpoint's body and response are the fn's args and result, and the hub's admin fn and the
web handler run the same operation. Each is tagged `admin`: never exposed through a hub MCP, run only for an admin,
and it waits for the person's approval unless a policy says otherwise.

```ts
const ADMIN_ENDPOINTS = {
  configure_provider: settingsSlice.endpoints.configureProvider,
  set_model_category: settingsSlice.endpoints.setModelCategory,
  set_budget: settingsSlice.endpoints.setBudget,
  set_priority: settingsSlice.endpoints.setPriority,
  set_policy: settingsSlice.endpoints.setPolicy,
  set_actor_setting: settingsSlice.endpoints.setActorSetting,
  add_user: settingsSlice.endpoints.addUser,
  add_group: settingsSlice.endpoints.addGroup,
  remove_rule: adminSlice.endpoints.removeRule,
  approve_pairing: systemsSlice.endpoints.approvePairing,
  create_pairing_token: systemsSlice.endpoints.createPairingToken,
  configure_system: systemsSlice.endpoints.configureSystem,
  enable_system: systemsSlice.endpoints.enableSystem,
  remove_system: systemsSlice.endpoints.removeSystem,
  install_system: systemsSlice.endpoints.installSystem,
  start_system: systemsSlice.endpoints.startSystem,
  stop_system: systemsSlice.endpoints.stopSystem,
  upgrade_system: systemsSlice.endpoints.upgradeSystem,
  set_model_role: modelsSlice.endpoints.setModelRole,
  clear_model_role: modelsSlice.endpoints.clearModelRole,
  set_model_limits: contextorSlice.endpoints.setModelLimits,
  create_mcp: mcpsSlice.endpoints.createMcp,
  update_mcp: mcpsSlice.endpoints.updateMcp,
  rotate_mcp_secret: mcpsSlice.endpoints.rotateMcpSecret,
  delete_mcp: mcpsSlice.endpoints.deleteMcp,
  preview_mcp: mcpsSlice.endpoints.previewMcp,
  install_package: packagingSlice.endpoints.installPackage,
  remove_instance: packagingSlice.endpoints.removeInstance,
  export_system_data: packagingSlice.endpoints.exportSystemData,
} as const satisfies Record<string, EndpointLike>;
```

#### `AdminFnName`

type · `import { AdminFnName } from '@harness/sdk'`

An admin fn's name.

```ts
type AdminFnName = keyof typeof ADMIN_ENDPOINTS;
```

#### `HUB_READ_ENDPOINTS`

const · `import { HUB_READ_ENDPOINTS } from '@harness/sdk'`

The web reads the hub also offers as fns, so the chat agent can look at everything a person can: fn name →
endpoint. Each is tagged `read`; the hub runs the same operation as the endpoint's handler, as the run's person.

```ts
const HUB_READ_ENDPOINTS = {
  resource_list: resourcesSlice.endpoints.browseResources,
  resource_read: resourcesSlice.endpoints.readResource,
  context_get: contextSlice.endpoints.contextItem,
  types_get: chatSlice.endpoints.types,
  runs_list: adminSlice.endpoints.runs,
  run_get: runsSlice.endpoints.run,
  run_tree: runsSlice.endpoints.tree,
  llm_call: runsSlice.endpoints.llmCall,
  model_calls: metricsSlice.endpoints.modelCalls,
  fn_metrics: metricsSlice.endpoints.fnMetrics,
  sessions_list: chatSlice.endpoints.sessions,
  session_messages: chatSlice.endpoints.messages,
  systems_list: systemsSlice.endpoints.systems,
  system_get: systemsSlice.endpoints.system,
  system_fns: adminSlice.endpoints.systemFns,
  system_logs: adminSlice.endpoints.systemLogs,
  model_roles: modelsSlice.endpoints.modelRoles,
  model_limits: contextorSlice.endpoints.modelLimits,
  /** Any list by the query standard (`LIST_FIELDS`): filters, sort, paging. */
  query: querySlice.endpoints.query,
} as const satisfies Record<string, EndpointLike>;
```

#### `HubReadFnName`

type · `import { HubReadFnName } from '@harness/sdk'`

A hub read fn's name.

```ts
type HubReadFnName = keyof typeof HUB_READ_ENDPOINTS;
```

#### `HUB_WRITE_ENDPOINTS`

const · `import { HUB_WRITE_ENDPOINTS } from '@harness/sdk'`

The web writes on resources, context and memory the hub also offers as fns: fn name → endpoint. None is tagged
`read`, so unless a policy says otherwise each waits for the person's approval.

```ts
  resource_convert: resourcesSlice.endpoints.convertResource,
  resource_import: resourcesSlice.endpoints.importResource,
  resource_rename: resourcesSlice.endpoints.renameResource,
  resource_delete: resourcesSlice.endpoints.deleteResource,
  context_from_resource: resourcesSlice.endpoints.contextFromResource,
  remember: contextSlice.endpoints.remember,
  forget: contextSlice.endpoints.forget,
} as const satisfies Record<string, EndpointLike>;
```

#### `HubWriteFnName`

type · `import { HubWriteFnName } from '@harness/sdk'`

#### `FIND_KINDS`

const · `import { FIND_KINDS } from '@harness/sdk'`

What `hub/find` can look for: every kind of item a context link can point at (core's `LinkTarget`).

```ts
const FIND_KINDS: readonly ["fn", "context", "resource", "type"];
```

#### `LlmPayloadType`

const · `import { LlmPayloadType } from '@harness/sdk'`

The payload of a `hub/llm` fn: an agent's instructions, model, tools, context, mode and limits. Built by `agent()`.

```ts
const LlmPayloadType = g.obj({
  /** Handlebars; `{{args.x}}` reads the args. */
  instructions: g.text(),
  model: g.optional(ModelChoiceType),
  /** Qualified fn names. */
  tools: g.list(g.text()),
  /** Fns the agent may add to its toolset while running. */
  canLoad: g.or([
    g.literal('none'),
    g.obj({ fns: g.optional(g.list(g.text())), systems: g.optional(g.list(g.text())), tags: g.optional(g.list(g.text())) }),
  ]),
  maxTools: g.num({ min: 1, whole: true }),
  /** Qualified context names, always loaded. */
  context: g.list(g.text()),
  /** Qualified hook names to run on each user message. */
  hooks: g.list(g.text()),
  toolExecution: g.enum(['parallel', 'sequential']),
  maxIterations: g.num({ min: 1, whole: true }),
  /** How fns reach the model (strict tools or the static toolset); the hub's default (`tools`) when absent. A session may override it. */
  mode: g.optional(ChatModeType),
  /** What goes into each prompt (plain, suggest or graph); the hub's default (`plain`) when absent. A session may override it. */
  contextor: g.optional(ContextorType),
  /** The most tokens one answer may take (as core's `ModelLimits.maxOutputTokens`); the model's own limit when absent. */
  maxOutput: g.optional(ginAs(ModelLimits.shape.maxOutputTokens.unwrap())),
});
```

Refers to: [`ModelLimits`](#core-modellimits).

#### `ModelChoiceType`

const · `import { ModelChoiceType } from '@harness/sdk'`

Which model an agent or an LLM call uses: a model id, a category, or automatic selection by weights.

```ts
const ModelChoiceType: OrType<{ model: string; } | { category: string; } | { auto: { cost?: number | undefined; speed?: number | undefined; accuracy?: number | undefined; }; }>;
```

const · `import { ChatModeType } from '@harness/sdk'`

A chat mode's settings, as a gin type: core's `ChatModeConfig` input (defaults may be left out).

```ts
const ChatModeType: GinType<{ type: "tools"; } | { type: "static"; protocol?: "text" | "auto" | "native" | undefined; placement?: "system-end" | "tail" | undefined; loadDetail?: "signature" | "listed" | "full" | undefined; }>;
```

Refers to: [`ChatModeConfig`](#core-chatmodeconfig).

#### `ContextorType`

const · `import { ContextorType } from '@harness/sdk'`

```ts
const ContextorType = ginAs(ContextorConfig);
```

Refers to: [`ContextorConfig`](#core-contextorconfig).

const · `import { FnErrorType } from '@harness/sdk'`

A failure, as a gin type (the same shape as core's `FnError`).


#### `StoredFn`

const · `import { StoredFn } from '@harness/sdk'`

A fn as `hub/fn_get` gives it.

```ts
const StoredFn: ObjType<{ fn: string; kind: string; description: string; version: number; available: boolean; instructions?: string | undefined; payload?: z.core.util.JSONType | undefined; owner?: string | undefined; routing?: { to: "instance"; instance: string; } | { to: "any"; } | undefined; }>;
```

Refers to: [`FnRouting`](#core-fnrouting).

#### `SaveFnArgs`

const · `import { SaveFnArgs } from '@harness/sdk'`

`hub/save_fn`'s args.

```ts
const SaveFnArgs = g.obj({
  kind: g.field(g.text({ minLength: 1 }), { docs: 'The qualified kind (`input/form`): one of the calling system\'s own kinds.' }),
  name: g.field(g.text({ minLength: 1 }), { docs: 'Its local name (`trip_details`, `main.overdue_invoices`); the hub names it `<system>/<name>`.' }),
  description: g.text({ minLength: 1 }),
  instructions: g.optional(g.text()),
  payload: json(),
  examples: g.optional(g.list(ginAs(FnExample))),
  tags: g.optional(g.list(g.text())),
  share: g.field(g.optional(ginAs(ShareRule)), { docs: 'Who else may use it: private to the run\'s person when absent on a new fn; saving again keeps the current sharing when absent.' }),
  routing: g.optional(ginAs(FnRouting)),
  replace: g.field(g.optional(g.bool()), { docs: 'Save over the fn of this name, keeping the old version; otherwise a taken name is refused.' }),
});
```

Refers to: [`FnExample`](#core-fnexample), [`FnRouting`](#core-fnrouting), [`ShareRule`](#core-sharerule).

#### `SAVE_FN_TAKEN`

const · `import { SAVE_FN_TAKEN } from '@harness/sdk'`

The error code `hub/save_fn` fails with (class `caller`) when the name is taken and `replace` is not set.

```ts
const SAVE_FN_TAKEN: "fn-taken";
```

## 19. The server: serve and the implementation

`serve(contract, impl, options, host?)` (from `@harness/sdk/server`) connects to the hub, pairs the first time, sends the
manifest (or nothing when the hub already has this version of it), and serves the implementation over the link. It
resolves with a `RunningSystem` once it has started connecting; `running.ready()` resolves once it is connected,
configured and imported.

The implementation's type is `SystemImpl<(typeof contract)['defs']>`, computed from the contract. Each section is
**required** when the contract declares something there and **forbidden** when it doesn't:

| Implementation section | One entry per | Shape |
|---|---|---|
| `fns` | native fn (not agents or fns of a kind) | `{ run(args, ctx), undo?(payload, ctx) }`; `undo` exactly when the fn declares `undo` |
| `types` | named type with methods | `{ <method>: { run(self, args, ctx), undo? } }` |
| `kinds` | kind | `{ check, describe, run, undo? }` |
| `context` | dynamic context (no `content`) | `async (args, ctx) => string` |
| `hooks` | hook | `async (input, ctx) => HookContribution[]` |
| `mentions` | mention provider | `{ search, hover, llm }` |
| `embedders` | embedder | `async (texts) => number[][]` |
| `sectioners` | sectioner | `async (text, stat) => Section[]` |
| `actions` | action | `async (input, ctx) => result` |
| `resources` | (optional) | a `ResourceProvider` |
| `events` | (optional) hub event type it handles | `async (event, ctx) => void` |
| `lifecycle` | (optional) moment | `async (ctx) => void` |

Build the implementation in a function that takes its dependencies (`createNotesImpl(deps)`), so `main.ts` passes the
real ones and tests pass fakes:

_From `scripts/docs/example/server/impl.ts`:_

```ts
/** Everything the implementation is built from. */
export interface NotesDeps {
  readonly settings: NotesSettings;
  /** Opens an instance's store from its data folder. */
  readonly open: OpenStore;
  readonly now: () => Date;
  /** A new note id. */
  readonly newId: () => string;
}
```

The process, `server/main.ts`: parse the env slice, build the dependencies and the implementation, optionally build
the renderers, and serve. `loggedHost(name)` gives the real host with structured logging and its logger.

_From `scripts/docs/example/server/main.ts`:_

```ts
const settings = notesEnv.parse();
const { host, log } = loggedHost(notes.name);

const impl = createNotesImpl({
  settings,
  open: storesBy((dataDir) => new JsonNoteStore(dataDir)),
  now: () => new Date(),
  newId: () => randomUUID(),
});

// the renderers, built into the state folder and uploaded to the hub on connect
const clientFolder = join(settings.HARNESS_SYSTEM_STATE, 'client');
await buildClient({ entry: fileURLToPath(new URL('../client/index.ts', import.meta.url)), outDir: clientFolder });

const running = await serve(notes, impl, {
  hub: settings.HARNESS_HUB,
  state: settings.HARNESS_SYSTEM_STATE,
  client: clientFolder,
  ...(settings.HARNESS_PAIRING_TOKEN === undefined ? {} : { pairingToken: settings.HARNESS_PAIRING_TOKEN }),
}, host);
running.onStatus((status) => { log.info('notes.status', { status }); });

const stop = () => {
  running.stop().finally(() => { process.exit(0); }).catch(() => undefined);
};
process.on('SIGINT', stop);
process.on('SIGTERM', stop);
```

**Options** (`ServeOptions`): `hub` (the link URL, `ws://<host>:4300/link`), `state` (the folder for its key pair and
outcome journal), `pairingToken` (a one-time token an admin made), `client` (the folder of the built renderers),
`version` (default: from `package.json`), `mcp` (also serve its native fns as an MCP server over stdio or HTTP),
`connectTimeout`, and seams for `files` and `fetch`.

**Env.** A system's tunables are an `@ayepi/env` slice that spreads `systemFields()` (the hub URL `HARNESS_HUB`, the
instance `HARNESS_INSTANCE`, the pairing token `HARNESS_PAIRING_TOKEN`, the data and cache folders) and its own, each a
zod schema with a default and a TSDoc line, then adds `systemState('<name>')`: the state folder
`HARNESS_SYSTEM_STATE`, which defaults to one of its own per instance. Feature code never reads `process.env`:

_From `scripts/docs/example/server/env.ts`:_

```ts
export const notesEnv = env({
  ...systemFields(),
  /** The most notes `find` returns when no limit is given. */
  NOTES_FIND_LIMIT: z.number().int().min(1).default(10),
  /** The most pinned titles the `pinned` hook adds to a message. */
  NOTES_HOOK_TITLES: z.number().int().min(0).default(5),
}).add(systemState('notes'));
```

**Pairing.** The first time a name connects, the hub has never seen it, so the system waits as `pairing`: it logs a
pairing code and link (and a QR code), and an admin approves it in the hub, filling in its config. With a pairing token
it pairs at once. Later starts prove the key kept in `state`. With a config type, the system waits as `needs-config`
until an admin sets a valid value; its fns are unavailable meanwhile.

**At runtime.** `running.add(name, fn({ … }), impl)` adds a native fn and returns its typed ref (a fn of a kind needs
no implementation); `running.remove(ref)` removes it; `running.restart(reason)` restarts the process (the hub imports
what changed); `running.onStatus(listener)` follows `connecting`, `pairing`, `needs-config`, `connected`,
`disconnected` and `stopped`; `running.stop()` stops it.

### Reference: the server: serve and the implementation

#### `serve`

function · `import { serve } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

Run a system: connect to the hub (pairing the first time), send the contract, and serve the implementation.
The implementation is checked against the contract at compile time; its `system.json`, when it has one, is checked
against the contract and this process's instance here (a mismatch throws `PackageManifestError` before anything
connects).

```ts
function serve<N extends string, C extends ContractShape>(contract: SystemContract<N, C>, impl: SystemImpl<C>, options: ServeOptions, host?: SystemHost): Promise<RunningSystem<N>>;
```

#### `ServeOptions`

interface · `import { ServeOptions } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

How a system process connects.

```ts
interface ServeOptions {
  /** The hub: its link URL (`ws://hub:4300/link`) or its own address (`http://hub:4300`), which becomes that link. */
  hub: string;
  /** The folder where the system keeps its key pair and outcome journal. */
  state: string;
  /**
   * The system's version. Defaults to its `system.json`'s, then to the version in the current folder's
   * `package.json`.
   */
  version?: string;
  /**
   * The folder holding the system's `system.json` (default: the current folder). When there is one, `serve` checks it
   * against the contract (the same name, a protocol this SDK speaks) before connecting, and takes its version.
   */
  package?: string;
  /** A one-time pairing token, if an admin made one. Otherwise the system logs a pairing code and link. */
  pairingToken?: string;
  /** The folder of the built client assets (`buildClient`'s output), if the system has renderers. */
  client?: string;
  /** Also serve the system's native fns as an MCP server. */
  mcp?: McpServe;
  /** After this long without a connection, the system logs that the hub is unreachable (it keeps retrying). Default 10 s. */
  connectTimeout?: Duration;
  /** The `ctx.files` each run gets. Default: the local disk, relative to the current folder. */
  files?: FilesFactory;
  /** The `ctx.fetch` each run gets. Default: the global `fetch`. */
  fetch?: typeof fetch;
}
```

Refers to: [`Duration`](#core-duration).

#### `McpServe`

type · `import { McpServe } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

Where the MCP face listens: stdin/stdout, or MCP Streamable HTTP on a port (any path; body limit `HARNESS_MCP_MAX_BODY`).

```ts
type McpServe = { stdio: true } | { http: { port: number; host?: string } };
```

#### `mcpToolNameOf`

function · `import { mcpToolNameOf } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

A fn's MCP tool name: `<system>_<local>` in the characters MCP allows (`mcpToolName`; a system name never holds
`_`). One name for a fn wherever an MCP offers it: a system's own MCP face and the hub's MCPs.

```ts
function mcpToolNameOf(fn: QualifiedName): string;
```

Refers to: [`QualifiedName`](#core-qualifiedname).

#### `RunningSystem`

interface · `import { RunningSystem } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

A running system.

```ts
interface RunningSystem<N extends string> extends FnSearch {
  readonly name: N;
  readonly status: RunningStatus;
  /** Calls `listener` on every status change; returns the unsubscribe. */
  onStatus(listener: (status: RunningStatus) => void): () => void;
  /** Resolves once connected, configured and the contract is imported. */
  ready(): Promise<void>;
  /** Add a native fn at runtime. Returns its ref. */
  add<const K extends string, T extends FnTypes & { kind: 'native' }>(name: K, def: FnDef<T>, impl: FnImpl<T>): Promise<FnRef<QualifiedName<N, K>, T['args'], T['result'], T['handlers']>>;
  /** Add a fn of some kind at runtime (no implementation: its kind runs it). */
  add<const K extends string, T extends FnTypes>(name: K, def: FnDef<T>): Promise<FnRef<QualifiedName<N, K>, T['args'], T['result'], T['handlers']>>;
  /** Remove a fn added at runtime. */
  remove(fn: AnyFnRef): Promise<void>;
  /**
   * Add or replace types at runtime (a database's table types, each with its `instance`) and remove types added this
   * way, by local name. While offline they ride in the next manifest. Throws when the hub refuses one.
   */
  types(upsert: readonly TypeDecl[], remove: readonly string[]): Promise<void>;
  /** Restart the process. It reconnects and the hub imports what changed. */
  restart(reason: string): Promise<never>;
  stop(): Promise<void>;
}
```

Refers to: [`QualifiedName`](#core-qualifiedname), [`TypeDecl`](#core-typedecl).

#### `RunningStatus`

type · `import { RunningStatus } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

Where a running system is.

```ts
type RunningStatus = 'connecting' | 'pairing' | 'needs-config' | 'connected' | 'disconnected' | 'stopped';
```

#### `SystemImpl`

type · `import { SystemImpl } from '@harness/sdk'`

A system's implementation, computed from its contract. It must implement exactly what the contract declares: every
native fn, every method of its types, every kind, every dynamic context, hook, mention provider, embedder,
sectioner and action.

```ts
type SystemImpl<C extends ContractShape> =
Needs<'fns', { readonly [K in NativeFnKeys<C['fns']>]: FnImpl<TypesOf<C['fns'][K]>, ConfigOf<C>> }> &
Needs<'kinds', { readonly [K in keyof C['kinds']]: C['kinds'][K] extends KindDef<infer P> ? KindImpl<P, ConfigOf<C>> : never }> &
Needs<'context', { readonly [K in DynamicContextKeys<C['context']>]: C['context'][K] extends ContextDef<infer A, true> ? ContextImpl<A, ConfigOf<C>> : never }> &
Needs<'hooks', { readonly [K in keyof C['hooks']]: HookImpl<ConfigOf<C>> }> &
Needs<'mentions', { readonly [K in keyof C['mentions']]: C['mentions'][K] extends MentionDef<infer T, infer V> ? MentionImpl<T, V, ConfigOf<C>> : never }> &
Needs<'types', { readonly [K in TypesWithMethods<C['types']>]: C['types'][K] extends TypeDef<infer V, infer M extends Methods> ? { readonly [J in keyof M]: MethodImpl<V, TypesOf<M[J]>, ConfigOf<C>> } : never }> &
Needs<'embedders', { readonly [K in keyof C['embedders']]: EmbedderImpl }> &
Needs<'sectioners', { readonly [K in keyof C['sectioners']]: SectionerImpl }> &
Needs<'actions', { readonly [K in keyof C['actions']]: C['actions'][K] extends ActionDef<infer I, infer R, infer St> ? ActionImpl<I, R, St, ConfigOf<C>> : never }> &
Needs<'providers', { readonly [K in keyof C['providers']]: ProviderImpl<ConfigOf<C>> }> &
(C['connect'] extends ConnectDecl ? { readonly connect: ConnectImpl<ConfigOf<C>> } : { readonly connect?: never }) &
{
  /** Resources this system serves. */
  readonly resources?: ResourceProvider;
  /** Hub events to receive. */
  readonly events?: EventHandlers<ConfigOf<C>>;
  readonly lifecycle?: Lifecycle<ConfigOf<C>>;
};
```

Refers to: [`ConnectDecl`](#core-connectdecl).

#### `FnImpl`

type · `import { FnImpl } from '@harness/sdk'`

The implementation of a native fn. `undo` is required exactly when the fn declares an undo type; `preview` when it
declares `approval` with a preview (checked when the system serves): what a run would do, or why it can't go ahead.

```ts
type FnImpl<T extends FnTypes, Cfg = unknown> =
{ run(args: T['args'], ctx: FnCtx<T, Cfg>): Promise<Outcome<T>>; preview?(args: T['args'], ctx: PreviewCtx<Cfg>): Promise<PreviewAnswer> } &
([T['undo']] extends [never] ? { undo?: never } : { undo(payload: T['undo'], ctx: UndoCtx<Cfg>): Promise<UndoOutcome> });
```

Refers to: [`PreviewAnswer`](#core-previewanswer), [`UndoOutcome`](#core-undooutcome).

#### `ConnectImpl`

interface · `import { ConnectImpl } from '@harness/sdk'`

A per-person system's connect flow, as the hub's connect page drives it. `start` gives the URL to send the person
to (OAuth; `state` must come back unchanged on the hub's redirect); `finish` turns what came back (the redirect's
query, or the form) into the account and credentials, or a problem in words; `disconnect` revokes the credentials
where the provider can (best effort).

```ts
interface ConnectImpl<Cfg> {
  start(request: ConnectStart, ctx: SystemCtx<Cfg>): Promise<{ url: string }> | { url: string };
  finish(request: ConnectFinish, ctx: SystemCtx<Cfg>): Promise<ConnectResult>;
  disconnect(connection: Pick<RunConnection, 'id' | 'owner' | 'credentials'>, ctx: SystemCtx<Cfg>): Promise<void>;
}
```

Refers to: [`ConnectFinish`](#core-connectfinish), [`ConnectResult`](#core-connectresult), [`ConnectStart`](#core-connectstart), [`RunConnection`](#core-runconnection).

#### `loggedHost`

function · `import { loggedHost } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

A `LinkHost` and the process log it will use, made before `serve` so the parts a system builds ahead of it (HTTP
clients, watchers) log through the same `@ayepi/log` logger: structured, sanitized lines on the console at once,
and to the hub while connected. Pass `host` to `serve`.

```ts
function loggedHost(name: string, deps?: Omit<LinkHostDeps, "logger">, output?: "stdout" | "stderr"): LoggedHost;
```

#### `LoggedHost`

interface · `import { LoggedHost } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

A link host and the process log it serves with.

```ts
interface LoggedHost {
  readonly host: LinkHost;
  /** The system's process log, with `system` on every line. */
  readonly log: Log;
}
```

#### `systemFields`

function · `import { systemFields } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

The link fields of a system's env slice, for spreading into it, followed by its state folder in a group of its own
(`systemState`): `env({ ...systemFields(), FS_SHELL: … }).add(systemState('fs'))`. The hub defaults to this
machine's default port, the instance to `main`.

```ts
function systemFields(): { readonly HARNESS_SYSTEM_DATA: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>; readonly HARNESS_SYSTEM_CACHE: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>; readonly HARNESS_HUB: z.ZodDefault<z.ZodString>; readonly HARNESS_INSTANCE: z.ZodDefault<z.core.$ZodBranded<z.ZodString, "InstanceName", "out">>; readonly HARNESS_PAIRING_TOKEN: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>; };
```

#### `systemState`

function · `import { systemState } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

A system's state folder, where it keeps its key pair, outcome journal and anything else it stores: the one named
(the hub's supervisor and `npm run dev` always name one), else `defaultStateFolder`, so two instances started by
hand on one machine never share a key pair. A group of its own because it reads the instance (`systemFields`).

```ts
function systemState(name: string): { readonly HARNESS_SYSTEM_STATE: (e: { readonly [SYSTEM_ENV.instance]: InstanceName; }) => z.ZodDefault<z.ZodString>; };
```

#### `defaultStateFolder`

function · `import { defaultStateFolder } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

The state folder an instance gets when none is named: `.harness/systems/<name>` for `main`, else `/<instance>` in it.

```ts
function defaultStateFolder(name: string, instance: InstanceName): string;
```

Refers to: [`InstanceName`](#core-instancename).

#### `SYSTEM_ENV`

const · `import { SYSTEM_ENV } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

The variables that tell a system process where its hub is, where to keep its state, and whether the hub supervises
it. The hub's supervisor sets them for systems it installs; `npm run dev` sets them for the dev systems. One record,
so the names can't drift.

```ts
const SYSTEM_ENV = {
  hub: 'HARNESS_HUB',
  state: 'HARNESS_SYSTEM_STATE',
  /** Which copy of the system this process is (`main` when unset); a fleet's instances each have their own. */
  instance: 'HARNESS_INSTANCE',
  /** The instance's own data folder, outside the package, kept across upgrades and exported with its data. */
  data: 'HARNESS_SYSTEM_DATA',
  /** The instance's cache folder: rebuildable, never exported. */
  cache: 'HARNESS_SYSTEM_CACHE',
  name: 'HARNESS_SYSTEM_NAME',
  pairingToken: 'HARNESS_PAIRING_TOKEN',
  supervised: 'HARNESS_SUPERVISED',
  restartCode: 'HARNESS_RESTART_CODE',
} as const;
```

#### `placeFolders`

function · `import { placeFolders } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

An instance's data and cache folders, as absolute paths: the ones the hub named, else `data` and `cache` in its
state folder. The SDK gives these to `ctx.dataDir` and `ctx.cacheDir`; a system that needs a folder before it
serves (to build its renderers into the cache) asks here, so the two never disagree.

```ts
function placeFolders(state: string, named: { readonly [SYSTEM_ENV.data]?: string | undefined; readonly [SYSTEM_ENV.cache]?: string | undefined; }): { dataDir: string; cacheDir: string; };
```

const · `import { sdkEnv } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

```ts
const sdkEnv: typeof sdkEnv; // too large to print: see its section
```

Refers to: [`InstanceName`](#core-instancename).

type · `import { SdkEnv } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

const · `import { RESTART_CODE } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

#### `SystemHost`

interface · `import { SystemHost } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

Runs systems. `LinkHost` is the real one; tests pass their own.

```ts
interface SystemHost {
  start<N extends string, C extends ContractShape>(contract: SystemContract<N, C>, impl: SystemImpl<C>, options: ServeOptions): Promise<RunningSystem<N>>;
```

#### `LinkHost`

class · `import { LinkHost } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

Runs systems over the WebSocket link.

```ts
class LinkHost implements SystemHost {
  constructor(deps: LinkHostDeps = {});
  start<N extends string, C extends ContractShape>(contract: SystemContract<N, C>, impl: SystemImpl<C>, options: ServeOptions): Promise<RunningSystem<N>>;
```

#### `LinkHostDeps`

interface · `import { LinkHostDeps } from '@harness/sdk/server'`

The seams `LinkHost` runs on. Every one has a real default; tests replace them.

```ts
interface LinkHostDeps {
  /** Opens the socket to the hub. Default: a real WebSocket with backoff and heartbeat. */
  readonly transport?: LinkTransport;
  /** The key store for a state folder. Default: `key.json` in it. */
  readonly keys?: (state: string) => KeyStore;
  /** The outcome journal for a state folder. Default: `journal.jsonl` in it. */
  readonly journal?: (state: string, size: number) => OutcomeJournal;
  readonly process?: ProcessControl;
  readonly now?: () => number;
  /** Stop on SIGINT / SIGTERM / SIGBREAK. Default true. */
  readonly signals?: boolean;
  /** The SDK tunables. Default: `sdkEnv`, read from the environment. */
  readonly env?: SdkEnv;
  /** The process logger. Default: console, plus the hub while connected. */
  readonly logger?: (sink: (line: LogLine) => void) => Logger;
  /** Shows a pending pairing. Default: a QR code on a terminal (`HARNESS_PAIRING_QR`), else a log line. */
  readonly pairing?: PairingDisplay;
  /** Makes the instance's data and cache folders before the system starts. Default: `mkdir -p` each. */
  readonly folders?: (dirs: readonly string[]) => Promise<void>;
}
```

Refers to: [`LogLine`](#core-logline).
